---
title: "digitalocean_app_response"
description: "`digitalocean_app_response` looks up a real, already-created App Platform application -- its full spec (services, jobs, static sites..."
---

`digitalocean_app_response` looks up a real, already-created App Platform application -- its full spec (services, jobs, static sites, and their build/deploy configuration) and current status.

## Example

<Tabs>
  <Tab title="Go">
    ```go
    // ubx sdk gen --only digitalocean --lang go --out ./local-sdk
    // go.mod: replace github.com/ubiquex/ubx-sdk-digitalocean/sdk/go => ./local-sdk/digitalocean/sdk/go
    package main

    import (
    	apps "github.com/ubiquex/ubx-sdk-digitalocean/sdk/go/digitalocean/apps"
    	ubx "github.com/ubiquex/ubx-sdk-go/runtime"
    )

    func main() {
    	ubx.Main(ubx.Stack("example", func() {
    		ubx.Intent(ubx.IntentInfo{Summary: "example own app response"})

    		ubx.Resource(apps.AppResponse, "example", apps.AppResponseConfig{
    			Id:        "example",
    			Spec:      map[string]any{"name": "example-name"},
    			ProjectId: "example",
    		})
    	}))
    }
    ```
  </Tab>
  <Tab title="TypeScript">
    ```typescript
    // ubx sdk gen --only digitalocean --lang ts --out ./local-sdk
    import { intent, resource, stack } from "@ubx/sdk";
    import { AppResponse } from "./local-sdk/digitalocean/sdk/typescript/digitalocean/apps/app_response.ts";

    export default stack("example", () => {
      intent({ summary: "example own app response" });

      resource(AppResponse, "example", {
        id: "example",
        spec: { name: "example-name" },
        projectId: "example",
      });
    });
    ```
  </Tab>
  <Tab title="Python">
    ```python
    # ubx sdk gen --only digitalocean --lang py --out ./local-sdk
    # export PYTHONPATH=./local-sdk/digitalocean/sdk/python:$PYTHONPATH
    import ubx_sdk as ubx
    from ubx.digitalocean.apps import AppResponse, AppResponseConfig
    from ubx.digitalocean.apps.app_response import AppResponse_App_ActiveDeployment_Spec

    def describe():
        ubx.intent("example own app response")

        ubx.resource(AppResponse, "example", AppResponseConfig(
            id="example",
            spec=AppResponse_App_ActiveDeployment_Spec(name="example-name"),
            project_id="example",
        ))

    if __name__ == "__main__":
        ubx.run("example", describe)
    ```
  </Tab>
  <Tab title="Markdown">
    ```
    Create digitalocean_app_response, with id example, spec a set
    value, project id example.
    ```
  </Tab>
</Tabs>

## Input properties

<ResponseField name="id" type="string" required>
  path parameter, not part of the API's own resource representation Required.
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="project_id" type="string">
  The ID of the project the app should be assigned to. If omitted, it will be assigned to your default project. &lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Requires `project:assign_resource` scope. Optional.
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="spec" type="object" required>
  The desired configuration of an application. Required.
  <Expandable title="properties">
    <ResponseField name="databases" type="list(object)">
      Database instances which can provide persistence to workloads within the application. Optional.
      <Expandable title="properties">
        <ResponseField name="cluster_name" type="string">
          Optional; if omitted, computed by DigitalOcean.
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="db_name" type="string">
          Optional; if omitted, computed by DigitalOcean.
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="db_user" type="string">
          Optional; if omitted, computed by DigitalOcean.
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="engine" type="string">
          Optional; if omitted, computed by DigitalOcean.
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="name" type="string">
          Optional; if omitted, computed by DigitalOcean.
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="production" type="bool">
          Optional; if omitted, computed by DigitalOcean.
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="version" type="string">
          Optional; if omitted, computed by DigitalOcean.
        </ResponseField>
      </Expandable>
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="disable_edge_cache" type="bool">
      If set to `true`, the app will **not** be cached at the edge (CDN). Enable this option if you want to manage CDN configuration yourself -- whether by using an external CDN provider or by handling static content and caching within your app. This setting is also recommended for apps that require real-time data or serve dynamic content, such as those using Server-Sent Events (SSE) over GET, or hosting an MCP (Model Context Protocol) Server that utilizes SSE. **Note:** This feature is not available for static site components. For more information, see [Disable CDN Cache](https://docs.digitalocean.com/products/app-platform/how-to/cache-content/#disable-cdn-cache). Optional.
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="disable_email_obfuscation" type="bool">
      If set to `true`, email addresses in the app will not be obfuscated. This is useful for apps that require email addresses to be visible (in the HTML markup). Optional.
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="domains" type="list(object)">
      A set of hostnames where the application will be available. Optional.

    <ResponseField name="maintenance" type="object">
      Specification to configure maintenance settings for the app, such as maintenance mode and archiving the app. Optional.
      <Expandable title="properties">
        <ResponseField name="archive" type="bool">
          Indicates whether the app should be archived. Setting this to true implies that enabled is set to true. Optional.
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="enabled" type="bool">
          Indicates whether maintenance mode should be enabled for the app. Optional.
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="offline_page_url" type="string">
          A custom offline page to display when maintenance mode is enabled or the app is archived. Optional.
        </ResponseField>
      </Expandable>
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="name" type="string" required>
      The name of the app. Must be unique across all apps in the same account. Required.

            <ResponseField name="disable_edge_cache" type="bool">
              If set to `true`, the app will **not** be cached at the edge (CDN). Enable this option if you want to manage CDN configuration yourself -- whether by using an external CDN provider or by handling static content and caching within your app. This setting is also recommended for apps that require real-time data or serve dynamic content, such as those using Server-Sent Events (SSE) over GET, or hosting an MCP (Model Context Protocol) Server that utilizes SSE. **Note:** This feature is not available for static site components. For more information, see [Disable CDN Cache](https://docs.digitalocean.com/products/app-platform/how-to/cache-content/#disable-cdn-cache). Optional.
            </ResponseField>
            <ResponseField name="disable_email_obfuscation" type="bool">
              If set to `true`, email addresses in the app will not be obfuscated. This is useful for apps that require email addresses to be visible (in the HTML markup). Optional.

            <ResponseField name="disable_edge_cache" type="bool">
              If set to `true`, the app will **not** be cached at the edge (CDN). Enable this option if you want to manage CDN configuration yourself -- whether by using an external CDN provider or by handling static content and caching within your app. This setting is also recommended for apps that require real-time data or serve dynamic content, such as those using Server-Sent Events (SSE) over GET, or hosting an MCP (Model Context Protocol) Server that utilizes SSE. **Note:** This feature is not available for static site components. For more information, see [Disable CDN Cache](https://docs.digitalocean.com/products/app-platform/how-to/cache-content/#disable-cdn-cache). Optional.
            </ResponseField>
            <ResponseField name="disable_email_obfuscation" type="bool">
              If set to `true`, email addresses in the app will not be obfuscated. This is useful for apps that require email addresses to be visible (in the HTML markup). Optional.
            </ResponseField>

        <ResponseField name="disable_edge_cache" type="bool">
          If set to `true`, the app will **not** be cached at the edge (CDN). Enable this option if you want to manage CDN configuration yourself -- whether by using an external CDN provider or by handling static content and caching within your app. This setting is also recommended for apps that require real-time data or serve dynamic content, such as those using Server-Sent Events (SSE) over GET, or hosting an MCP (Model Context Protocol) Server that utilizes SSE. **Note:** This feature is not available for static site components. For more information, see [Disable CDN Cache](https://docs.digitalocean.com/products/app-platform/how-to/cache-content/#disable-cdn-cache). Optional.
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="disable_email_obfuscation" type="bool">
          If set to `true`, email addresses in the app will not be obfuscated. This is useful for apps that require email addresses to be visible (in the HTML markup). Optional.
